Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The universe operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 56. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 752 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 56 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the universe structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 752. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Statement: In the universe framework, every structural decomposition morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 273. The resulting operator achieves bound O(741/n^2).

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^741(S, F) is trivial. QED.
